def labelled_by_program(pid: str) -> list[dict]:
    return [q for q in LABELLED if q["program"] == pid]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# the library
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

class Library:
    """Versions, active pointers, proposals, and the economics of reuse."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.versions: dict[str, list[Program]] = {}
        self.active: dict[str, str] = {}
        self.proposals: list[dict] = []
        for prog in (PROC_V1, DPA_V1, EMERG_V1):
            self.add(prog, activate=True)
        self.proposals.append(dict(PROPOSAL_V2))

    # -- versions ----------------------------------------------------------

    def add(self, prog: Program, activate: bool = False) -> Program:
        lst = self.versions.setdefault(prog.id, [])
        lst.append(prog)
        lst.sort(key=lambda p: p.version)
        if activate or prog.id not in self.active:
            self.active[prog.id] = prog.version
        return prog

    def get(self, pid: str, version: str | None = None) -> Program | None:
        vs = self.versions.get(pid) or []
        if not vs:
            return None
        v = version or self.active.get(pid)
        for p in vs:
            if p.version == v:
                return p
        return vs[-1]

    def all_active(self) -> list[Program]:
        return [self.get(pid) for pid in self.versions]

    def public(self) -> list[dict]:
        out = []
        for pid, vs in self.versions.items():
            active = self.get(pid)
            out.append({
                "program": active.public(),
                "versions": [{"version": p.version, "verified": p.verified,
                              "build_cost": round(p.build_cost, 4),
                              "predicates": len(p.predicates)} for p in vs],
                "active": active.version,
                "proposals": [p for p in self.proposals if p["target"].split("@")[0] == pid],
                "labelled": len(labelled_by_program(pid)),
                "build_cost_total": round(sum(p.build_cost for p in vs), 4),
            })
        return sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["program"]["id"])

    # -- economics ---------------------------------------------------------

    def build_totals(self) -> dict:
        one_time = 0.0
        for vs in self.versions.values():
            for p in vs:
                one_time += p.build_cost
        return {"programs": len(self.versions), "versions": sum(len(v) for v in self.versions.values()),
                "one_time_cost": round(one_time, 4)}

    def apply_build_costs(self) -> None:
        for vs in self.versions.values():
            for p in vs:
                if not p.build_cost:
                    p.build_cost = build_cost(p.build_tokens_in, p.build_tokens_out)


def default_library() -> Library:
    lib = Library()
    lib.apply_build_costs()
    return lib



================================================
FILE: reuse.py
================================================
"""
jig.reuse — does the structure survive new facts?

Building a program costs one frontier-scale authoring pass. Reusing it costs a
planner call and a few judgments. That only matters if reuse is real, so this
module tests the two ways it can fail to be:

  1. the cost saving has to persist on questions the author never saw;
  2. the facts have to be refreshed without rebuilding the program — a vendor
     suspended, a budget topped up, a policy threshold moved, a memo withdrawn.

Every variant below is staged as a corpus edit and re-run through the *same*
program version. Nothing is rebuilt. What changes is which facts the predicates
see, which is the point: the structure is the reusable object, the facts are
not.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from .corpus import Corpus, Doc, DEFAULT
from .core import frontier_cost
from .engine import Engine
from .programs import LABELLED

# Questions the programs were authored against.
BUILD_SET = ["q01", "q02", "q05", "q11", "q14"]
HELD_OUT = [q["id"] for q in LABELLED if q["id"] not in BUILD_SET]


def by_id(qid: str) -> dict:
    return next(q for q in LABELLED if q["id"] == qid)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# savings curve
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def savings(econ: dict, ns=(1, 2, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250, 500)) -> list[dict]:
    f = econ["frontier_per_question"]
    r = econ["reused_per_question"]
    build = econ["one_time_build_cost"]
    rows = []
    for n in ns:
        front = f * n
        reused = build + r * n
        rows.append({"n": n, "frontier": round(front, 3), "reused": round(reused, 3),
                     "marginal": round(r * n, 4),
                     "saving": round(front - reused, 3),
                     "ratio": round(front / reused, 2) if reused else None})
    return rows


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# fact-change variants
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _top_up_sales(c: Corpus) -> Corpus:
    c = c.replace_text("registry/budget", "SALES-TOOLS — remaining $0, status closed.",
                       "SALES-TOOLS — remaining $8,000, status open.")
    c = c.replace_text("policy/procurement-2.0",
                       "Purchases with a total value up to $5,000 may be",
                       "Purchases with a total value up to $7,500 may be")
    return c


def _nimbus_remediated(c: Corpus) -> Corpus:
    return c.replace_text("registry/security",
                          "Nimbus Hosting — review completed 2024-09-01, status expired.",
                          "Nimbus Hosting — review completed 2026-10-20, status current.")


def _memo_withdrawn(c: Corpus) -> Corpus:
    return c.remove("memo/conference-exception")


def _addendum_lapsed(c: Corpus) -> Corpus:
    return c.remove("contracts/msa-orbit")


VARIANTS = [
    dict(id="fact-budget", title="Budget topped up, limit raised to $7,500",
         note="Two fiscal facts change. The program does not: the threshold "
              "predicate re-reads the policy and the budget predicate re-reads "
              "the register.",
         setup=_top_up_sales, expect={"q03": "APPROVE"}, as_of=None),
    dict(id="fact-vendor", title="Nimbus passes its security review",
         note="The same vendor, the same question, one register line replaced.",
         setup=_nimbus_remediated, expect={"q02": "APPROVE"}, as_of=None),
    dict(id="fact-memo", title="The conference pre-approval memo is withdrawn",
         note="An exception disappearing must not crash the program; the "
              "exception predicate simply stops finding cover.",
         setup=_memo_withdrawn, expect={"q05": "DENY", "q06": "DENY"}, as_of=None),
    dict(id="fact-contract", title="Orbit's addendum lapses",
         note="Clearance flips on a contract rather than a register.",
         setup=_addendum_lapsed, expect={"q11": "BLOCKED"}, as_of=None),
    dict(id="rule-reverted", title="Ask everything as of 2026-05-01, before v2.0",
         note="No corpus change at all: only the date moves, so the version in "
              "force moves with it. This is the case a cached answer gets wrong.",
         setup=None, expect={"q01": "DENY", "q07": "DENY"}, as_of="2026-05-01"),
]


def run_variants(engine: Engine) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for v in VARIANTS:
        corpus = v["setup"](engine.corpus.clone()) if v["setup"] else engine.corpus
        rows = []
        for q in LABELLED:
            slots = {"as_of": v["as_of"]} if v["as_of"] else None
            d = engine.run(q["question"], corpus=corpus, slots_override=slots)
            base = engine.run(q["question"])
            rows.append({"id": q["id"], "before": base.label, "after": d.label,
                         "changed": base.label != d.label,
                         "expect_after": v["expect"].get(q["id"]),
                         "expect_ok": (None if q["id"] not in v["expect"]
                                       else d.label == v["expect"][q["id"]])})
        changed = [r for r in rows if r["changed"]]
        checks = [r for r in rows if r["expect_after"]]
        out.append({
            "id": v["id"], "title": v["title"], "note": v["note"],
            "rows": rows, "changed": changed,
            "checks_passed": sum(1 for r in checks if r["expect_ok"]),
            "checks_total": len(checks),
            "pass": all(r["expect_ok"] for r in checks),
            "rebuilt": False,
            "build_cost": 0.0,
            "per_run_cost": round(sum(engine.run(q["question"], corpus=corpus,
                                                 slots_override=(
                                                     {"as_of": v["as_of"]}
                                                     if v["as_of"] else None)
                                                 ).cost["total"]
                                     for q in LABELLED) / len(LABELLED), 6),
        })
    return out


def experiment(engine: Engine, econ: dict) -> dict:
    variants = run_variants(engine)
    held = [by_id(qid) for qid in HELD_OUT]
    runs = [engine.run(q["question"]) for q in held]
    answered = [r for r in runs if r.verdict != "ESCALATE"]
    held_cost = (sum(r.cost["total"] for r in answered) / max(1, len(answered)))
    escalated = [r for r in runs if r.verdict == "ESCALATE"]
    build = econ["one_time_build_cost"]
    return {
        "build_set": BUILD_SET,
        "held_out": HELD_OUT,
        "held_out_cost_per_answered": round(held_cost, 6),
        "held_out_escalations": len(escalated),
        "held_out_escalation_cost": round(sum(r.cost["total"] for r in escalated), 4),
        "frontier_per_question": round(frontier_cost(), 4),
        "held_out_ratio": round(frontier_cost() / held_cost, 1) if held_cost else None,
        "one_time_build_cost": build,
        "build_cost_per_program": round(build / max(1, econ["programs"]), 4),
        "savings": savings(econ),
        "variants": variants,
        "variants_passed": sum(1 for v in variants if v["pass"]),
        "variants_total": len(variants),
        "rebuilds_required": 0,
    }



================================================
FILE: server.py
================================================
"""
jig.server — the HTTP surface. Standard library only.

State is a single engine instance; everything expensive is computed on request
and cached for the life of the process. /api/reset rebuilds the library so the
whole narrative (unverified v1.0 -> proposal -> verified v2.0) can be replayed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs

from .bench import economics, localise, run_all
from .core import RATES, frontier_cost
from .corpus import DEFAULT
from .engine import Engine
from .judge import get_judge
from .programs import LABELLED, PROPOSAL_V2, default_library
from .reuse import experiment as reuse_experiment
from .verify import (apply_proposal, before_after, program_interventions,
                     run_intervention, verify_all, verify_program)

WEB = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "web")

STATE_LOCK = threading.Lock()


class State:
    def __init__(self):
        self.reset()

    def reset(self):
        self.engine = Engine(library=default_library(), corpus=DEFAULT, judge=get_judge())
        self._bench = None
        self._reuse = None
        self._verify = None

    # -- cached computations ----------------------------------------------

    def bench(self):
        if self._bench is None:
            arms = run_all(self.engine)
            self._bench = {
                "arms": arms,
                "economics": economics(self.engine, arms),
                "localisation": [localise(self.engine, q) for q in LABELLED],
            }
        return self._bench

    def reuse(self):
        if self._reuse is None:
            self._reuse = reuse_experiment(self.engine, self.bench()["economics"])
        return self._reuse

    def verify(self):
        if self._verify is None:
            results = verify_all(self.engine)
            self._verify = {"programs": results, "before_after": before_after(self.engine)}
        return self._verify

    def invalidate(self):
        self._bench = self._reuse = self._verify = None


STATE = State()


def state_payload() -> dict:
    eng = STATE.engine
    j = eng.judge
    return {
        "library": eng.library.public(),
        "build": eng.library.build_totals(),
        "proposals": eng.library.proposals,
        "corpus": {"documents": len(eng.corpus.docs),
                   "documents_detail": eng.corpus.public()},
        "labelled": LABELLED,
        "rates": RATES,
        "frontier_cost": round(frontier_cost(), 4),
        "judge": {"name": j.name, "is_model": j.is_model,
                  "available": getattr(j, "available", False),
                  "note": ("model-backed judge" if j.is_model else
                           "offline reference judge: entailment patterns over the "
                           "retrieved excerpts, deterministic")},
        "labels": {
            "cost_model": "MODELLED — declared token rates and call counts; the call "
                          "counts are real, the prices and latencies are the "
                          "published-rate model in core.RATES",
            "frontier_arm": "MODELLED — the frontier arm's answers are the labelled "
                            "ground truth, standing in for a real frontier run; its "
                            "cost and latency are computed from RATES",
            "corpus": "SYNTHETIC — a 15-document fixture written for this test, not "
                      "an organisation's real policy",
            "judge": "OFFLINE — judge predicates run on the reference evaluator here; "
                     "LLMJudge is the same interface wired to an OpenAI-compatible "
                     "endpoint when JIG_LLM_BASE is set",
            "error_budget": "Every metric is exact over the 15 labelled questions: "
                            "no sampling, no confidence intervals. The only "
                            "uncertainty is the labelling itself — each ground truth "
                            "is derivable from corpus.py and auditable in programs.py",
        },
    }


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype: str):
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def json(self, obj, code: int = 200):
        self._send(code, json.dumps(obj, default=str).encode(), "application/json")

    def do_OPTIONS(self):
        self.send_response(204)
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
        self.end_headers()

    def do_GET(self):
        u = urlparse(self.path)
        path, qs = u.path, parse_qs(u.query)
        try:
            if path in ("/", "/index.html", "/app.html"):
                return self.static("app.html")
            if path == "/api/health":
                return self.json({"ok": True, "programs": len(STATE.engine.library.versions)})
            if path == "/api/state":
                return self.json(state_payload())
            if path == "/api/corpus":
                return self.json({"documents": STATE.engine.corpus.public()})
            if path == "/api/library":
                return self.json({"library": STATE.engine.library.public(),
                                  "build": STATE.engine.library.build_totals(),
                                  "proposals": STATE.engine.library.proposals})
            if path == "/api/verify":
                return self.json(STATE.verify())
            if path == "/api/bench":
                return self.json(STATE.bench())
            if path == "/api/reuse":
                return self.json(STATE.reuse())
            if path == "/api/labelled":
                return self.json({"questions": LABELLED})
            if path.startswith("/api/program/"):
                pid = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
                ver = (qs.get("version") or [None])[0]
                prog = STATE.engine.library.get(pid, ver)
                if not prog:
                    return self.json({"error": "unknown program"}, 404)
                return self.json({"program": prog.public()})
            if path == "/api/interventions":
                ivs = []
                for pid in STATE.engine.library.versions:
                    prog = STATE.engine.library.get(pid)
                    for iv in program_interventions(pid):
                        ivs.append(run_intervention(STATE.engine, iv, prog))
                return self.json({"interventions": ivs})
            return self.json({"error": "not found"}, 404)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            return self.json({"error": str(e), "type": type(e).__name__}, 500)

    def do_POST(self):
        u = urlparse(self.path)
        n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
        try:
            body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            body = {}

        try:
            if u.path == "/api/decide":
                q = (body.get("question") or "").strip()
                if not q:
                    return self.json({"error": "question is required"}, 400)
                d = STATE.engine.run(
                    q,
                    program_id=body.get("program") or None,
                    version=body.get("version") or None,
                    targeted=body.get("targeted", True) is not False,
                    scoped=body.get("scoped", True) is not False,
                )
                return self.json({"decision": d.public(),
                                  "plan": d.trace[0].get("plan") if d.trace else None})
            if u.path == "/api/admit":
                pid = body.get("proposal") or PROPOSAL_V2["id"]
                prop = next((p for p in STATE.engine.library.proposals
                             if p["id"] == pid), PROPOSAL_V2)
                with STATE_LOCK:
                    res = apply_proposal(STATE.engine, prop)
                    STATE.invalidate()
                return self.json(res)
            if u.path == "/api/verify" and body.get("version"):
                prog = STATE.engine.library.get(body["program"], body["version"])
                if prog is None:
                    return self.json({"error": "unknown program version"}, 404)
                return self.json(verify_program(STATE.engine, prog))
            if u.path == "/api/reset":
                with STATE_LOCK:
                    STATE.reset()
                return self.json({"ok": True, "library": STATE.engine.library.public()})
            return self.json({"error": "not found"}, 404)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            return self.json({"error": str(e), "type": type(e).__name__}, 500)

    def static(self, name: str):
        path = os.path.join(WEB, name)
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return self.json({"error": "no ui"}, 404)
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            body = f.read()
        self._send(200, body, "text/html; charset=utf-8")


def main(port: int = 8010, host: str = "0.0.0.0"):
    srv = ThreadingHTTPServer((host, port), Handler)
    print(f"jig listening on http://{host}:{port}")
    srv.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    main(int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 8010)

"""
Tests for the decision-program pipeline.

Run: python3 -m unittest discover -s tests

The suite is deliberately exact: the corpus is a fixture, so every expected
verdict reduces to arithmetic over fixed documents. Nothing here asserts on
model behaviour, because no model is called.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sys
import threading
import unittest
import urllib.request
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from jig import core
from jig.bench import arm_single, economics, localise, run_all
from jig.core import AND, NOT, OR, P, FALSE, TRUE, UNKNOWN, build_cost, parse_expr
from jig.corpus import DEFAULT, Corpus, quote_for
from jig.engine import Engine, extract_slots
from jig.judge import (op_budget_line, op_dpa_on_file, op_manager_limit,
                       op_personal_data, op_security_review, op_vendor_status)
from jig.programs import (LABELLED, PROC_V2, PROPOSAL_V2, default_library,
                          labelled_by_program)
from jig.reuse import BUILD_SET, HELD_OUT, experiment as reuse_experiment
from jig.verify import (apply_proposal, before_after, run_intervention,
                        program_interventions, verify_all, verify_program)


def engine_with_v2():
    eng = Engine()
    apply_proposal(eng, PROPOSAL_V2)
    return eng


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- truth tables

class TestLogic(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_kleene_and(self):
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_and([TRUE, TRUE]), TRUE)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_and([TRUE, FALSE]), FALSE)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_and([FALSE, UNKNOWN]), FALSE)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_and([TRUE, UNKNOWN]), UNKNOWN)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_and([UNKNOWN, UNKNOWN]), UNKNOWN)

    def test_kleene_or(self):
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_or([FALSE, FALSE]), FALSE)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_or([TRUE, UNKNOWN]), TRUE)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_or([FALSE, UNKNOWN]), UNKNOWN)

    def test_kleene_not(self):
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_not(TRUE), FALSE)
        self.assertIs(core.kleene_not(UNKNOWN), UNKNOWN)

    def test_parser_precedence(self):
        e = parse_expr("P1 AND NOT P2 OR P3")
        self.assertEqual(e.evaluate({"P1": TRUE, "P2": FALSE, "P3": FALSE}), TRUE)
        self.assertEqual(e.evaluate({"P1": FALSE, "P2": FALSE, "P3": FALSE}), FALSE)

    def test_parser_parens(self):
        e = parse_expr("P1 AND (P2 OR P3)")
        self.assertEqual(e.evaluate({"P1": TRUE, "P2": FALSE, "P3": TRUE}), TRUE)
        self.assertEqual(e.evaluate({"P1": FALSE, "P2": TRUE, "P3": TRUE}), FALSE)

    def test_parser_preds(self):
        self.assertEqual(set(parse_expr("P6 OR (P1 AND P2 AND (NOT P3 OR P4))").preds()),
                         {"P1", "P2", "P3", "P4", "P6"})

    def test_unknown_propagates_only_when_it_matters(self):
        e = parse_expr("P1 AND P2")
        self.assertIs(e.evaluate({"P1": FALSE}), FALSE)
        self.assertIs(e.evaluate({"P1": TRUE}), UNKNOWN)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- corpus

class TestCorpus(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_version_in_force_by_date(self):
        self.assertEqual(DEFAULT.in_force("policy/procurement", "2026-06-30").id,
                         "policy/procurement-1.4")
        self.assertEqual(DEFAULT.in_force("policy/procurement", "2026-07-01").id,
                         "policy/procurement-2.0")
        self.assertIsNone(DEFAULT.in_force("policy/procurement", "2024-01-01"))

    def test_superseded_flag(self):
        old = DEFAULT.get("policy/procurement-1.4")
        new = DEFAULT.get("policy/procurement-2.0")
        self.assertTrue(DEFAULT.is_superseded(old, "2026-11-05"))
        self.assertFalse(DEFAULT.is_superseded(old, "2026-06-01"))
        self.assertFalse(DEFAULT.is_superseded(new, "2026-11-05"))

    def test_search_respects_scope(self):
        hits = DEFAULT.search("security review register", "2026-11-05",
                              only=["registry/security"], k=4)
        self.assertTrue(hits)
        self.assertTrue(all(h.doc_id.startswith("registry/security") for h in hits))

    def test_search_without_scope_can_reach_anything(self):
        hits = DEFAULT.search("conference travel pre-approved", "2026-11-05", only=None, k=3)
        self.assertTrue(any(h.doc_id == "memo/conference-exception" for h in hits))

    def test_corpus_is_frozen_under_clone(self):
        c = DEFAULT.remove("registry/security")
        self.assertIsNone(c.get("registry/security"))
        self.assertIsNotNone(DEFAULT.get("registry/security"))

    def test_quote_is_verbatim(self):
        doc = DEFAULT.get("registry/budget")
        q = quote_for(doc, ["frozen", "mkt-events"])
        self.assertIn(q, doc.text)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- operators

class TestOperators(unittest.TestCase):
    def ctx(self, **kw):
        base = dict(amount=4200.0, vendor="Acme Analytics", category="analytics",
                    budget_line="ENG-TOOLS", as_of="2026-11-05", event=None)
        base.update(kw)
        return base

    def ex(self, query, scope, as_of="2026-11-05"):
        return DEFAULT.search(query, as_of, only=scope, k=4)

    def test_manager_limit_boundary(self):
        from jig.programs import PROC_V2
        p = PROC_V2.predicate("P1")
        self.assertIs(op_manager_limit(p, self.ctx(amount=5000.0),
                                       self.ex("manager limit", ["policy/"]))
                      .answer, TRUE)
        self.assertIs(op_manager_limit(p, self.ctx(amount=5000.01),
                                       self.ex("manager limit", ["policy/"]))
                      .answer, FALSE)

    def test_manager_limit_follows_the_date(self):
        from jig.programs import PROC_V2
        p = PROC_V2.predicate("P1")
        # $4,200 is inside the v2.0 limit and outside the v1.4 limit
        self.assertIs(op_manager_limit(p, self.ctx(as_of="2026-11-05"),
                                       self.ex("manager limit", ["policy/"], "2026-11-05"))
                      .answer, TRUE)
        self.assertIs(op_manager_limit(p, self.ctx(as_of="2026-05-01"),
                                       self.ex("manager limit", ["policy/"], "2026-05-01"))
                      .answer, FALSE)

    def test_vendor_status_unknown_vendor_is_a_gap_not_a_false(self):
        p = PROC_V2.predicate("P2")
        r = op_vendor_status(p, self.ctx(vendor="Halcyon Data"),
                             self.ex("vendor register", ["registry/vendors"]))
        self.assertIs(r.answer, UNKNOWN)
        self.assertIn("Halcyon Data", r.gap)

    def test_security_review_expiry(self):
        p = PROC_V2.predicate("P4")
        r = op_security_review(p, self.ctx(vendor="Nimbus Hosting"),
                               self.ex("security review", ["registry/security"]))
        self.assertIs(r.answer, FALSE)
        # exactly 12 months is inside the window; 12.1 is not
        r2 = op_security_review(p, self.ctx(vendor="Acme Analytics", as_of="2027-02-20"),
                                self.ex("security review", ["registry/security"], "2026-11-05"))
        self.assertIs(r2.answer, TRUE)
        r3 = op_security_review(p, self.ctx(vendor="Acme Analytics", as_of="2027-02-21"),
                                self.ex("security review", ["registry/security"], "2026-11-05"))
        self.assertIs(r3.answer, FALSE)

    def test_budget_frozen(self):
        from jig.programs import PROC_V2
        p = PROC_V2.predicate("P5")
        r = op_budget_line(p, self.ctx(budget_line="MKT-EVENTS"),
                           self.ex("budget line", ["registry/budget"]))
        self.assertIs(r.answer, FALSE)
        self.assertIn("frozen", r.rationale)

    def test_dpa_must_match_the_vendor(self):
        from jig.programs import DPA_V1
        p = DPA_V1.predicate("P2")
        r = op_dpa_on_file(p, self.ctx(vendor="Ollie Labs"),
                           self.ex("data processing addendum", ["contracts/", "registry/vendors"]))
        self.assertIs(r.answer, FALSE)

    def test_personal_data_taxonomy(self):
        from jig.programs import DPA_V1
        p = DPA_V1.predicate("P1")
        self.assertIs(op_personal_data(p, self.ctx(category="hardware"),
                                       self.ex("personal data", ["policy/data-taxonomy"]))
                      .answer, FALSE)
        self.assertIs(op_personal_data(p, self.ctx(category="analytics"),
                                       self.ex("personal data", ["policy/data-taxonomy"]))
                      .answer, TRUE)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- engine

class TestEngine(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.eng = engine_with_v2()

    def run_q(self, qid):
        q = next(x for x in LABELLED if x["id"] == qid)
        return self.eng.run(q["question"]), q

    def test_q01_approves_with_four_citations(self):
        d, q = self.run_q("q01")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "APPROVE")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, q["expect"])
        self.assertEqual(sorted(c.doc_id for c in d.citations),
                         ["policy/data-taxonomy", "policy/procurement-2.0",
                          "registry/budget", "registry/security", "registry/vendors"])
        self.assertEqual(d.cost["judge_calls"], 0)

    def test_q02_denies_on_expired_review(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q02")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "DENY")
        p4 = next(f for f in d.findings if f.predicate == "P4")
        self.assertIs(p4.answer, FALSE)
        self.assertIn("registry/security", [c.doc_id for c in p4.citations])

    def test_early_stop_skips_dependent_predicates(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q03")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "DENY")
        skipped = {s["predicate"] for s in d.skipped}
        self.assertTrue({"P3", "P4", "P5"} <= skipped,
                        f"expected the security and budget predicates to be skipped, "
                        f"got {skipped}")
        self.assertLessEqual(d.evaluated, 2)

    def test_exception_rescues_a_frozen_budget(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q05")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "APPROVE")
        p5 = next(f for f in d.findings if f.predicate == "P5")
        self.assertIs(p5.answer, FALSE)
        p6 = next(f for f in d.findings if f.predicate == "P6")
        self.assertIs(p6.answer, TRUE)
        self.assertEqual(p6.evaluator, "judge")
        self.assertIn("memo/conference-exception", [c.doc_id for c in p6.citations])
        self.assertIn("events/calendar", [c.doc_id for c in p6.citations])

    def test_unknown_vendor_defers_with_the_open_question(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q09")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "DEFER")
        self.assertTrue(d.gaps)
        self.assertIn("Halcyon Data", " ".join(d.gaps))

    def test_out_of_scope_escalates_without_answering(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q10")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "ESCALATE")
        self.assertEqual(d.evaluated, 0)
        self.assertEqual(d.cost["frontier"], round(core.frontier_cost(), 5))

    def test_predicate_not_applicable_is_vacuous(self):
        # a conference request has no vendor, so the vendor predicates do not apply
        d, _ = self.run_q("q05")
        p2 = next(f for f in d.findings if f.predicate == "P2")
        self.assertEqual(p2.answer, "n/a")
        self.assertEqual(p2.citations, [])

    def test_dpa_early_stop_avoids_the_contract(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q12")
        self.assertEqual(d.label, "CLEARED")
        self.assertEqual(d.evaluated, 1)
        self.assertEqual([s["predicate"] for s in d.skipped], ["P2"])

    def test_cost_counts_judge_calls_only(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q05")
        self.assertEqual(d.cost["judge_calls"], 1)
        # the judge call is priced on the evidence it carried, which is what the
        # trace records for that predicate
        step = next(s for s in d.trace if s["step"] == "resolve" and s["predicate"] == "P6")
        expected = core.call_cost("planner") + core.call_cost(
            "judge", 1, docs=max(1, len(step["hits"])))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(d.cost["total"], expected, places=6)

    def test_trace_is_complete(self):
        d, _ = self.run_q("q01")
        steps = [s["step"] for s in d.trace]
        self.assertEqual(steps[0], "plan")
        self.assertIn("bind", steps)
        self.assertIn("combine", steps)
        resolves = [s for s in d.trace if s["step"] == "resolve"]
        self.assertEqual(len(resolves), d.evaluated)
        for s in resolves:
            self.assertIn("scope", s)
            self.assertIsInstance(s["hits"], list)

    def test_slots_are_grounded_from_the_question(self):
        s = extract_slots("Can we approve a $4,200 analytics subscription from Acme "
                          "Analytics on 2026-11-05, charged to ENG-TOOLS?", DEFAULT)
        self.assertEqual(s["amount"], 4200.0)
        self.assertEqual(s["vendor"], "Acme Analytics")
        self.assertEqual(s["category"], "analytics")
        self.assertEqual(s["budget_line"], "ENG-TOOLS")
        self.assertEqual(s["as_of"], "2026-11-05")

    def test_unscoped_retrieval_costs_more_than_scoped(self):
        q = next(x for x in LABELLED if x["id"] == "q02")["question"]
        scoped = self.eng.run(q, program=self.eng.library.get("PROC-01"))
        loose = self.eng.run(q, program=self.eng.library.get("PROC-01"), scoped=False)
        self.assertGreater(loose.cost["judge"], scoped.cost["judge"])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- verification

class TestInterventions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_v1_fails_the_missing_source_test(self):
        eng = Engine()
        v1 = eng.library.get("PROC-01", "1.0")
        r = verify_program(eng, v1)
        self.assertFalse(r["verified"])
        self.assertIn("iv-missing-01", r["failures"])
        self.assertIn("q02", r["failures"])

    def test_v1_keeps_answering_when_the_source_is_deleted(self):
        """The failure mode the intervention exists to catch."""
        eng = Engine()
        iv = next(i for i in program_interventions("PROC-01") if i.id == "iv-missing-01")
        r = run_intervention(eng, iv, eng.library.get("PROC-01", "1.0"))
        self.assertFalse(r["pass"])
        self.assertEqual(r["got"], "APPROVE")   # should have deferred

    def test_v2_passes_everything(self):
        eng = Engine()
        r = verify_program(eng, PROC_V2)
        self.assertTrue(r["verified"], r["failures"])
        self.assertEqual(r["interventions_passed"], 5)
        self.assertEqual(r["regression_total"], 9)
        self.assertEqual(r["regression_passed"], 9)

    def test_admission_changes_the_active_version(self):
        eng = Engine()
        self.assertEqual(eng.library.get("PROC-01").version, "1.0")
        res = apply_proposal(eng, PROPOSAL_V2)
        self.assertEqual(res["status"], "admitted")
        self.assertEqual(eng.library.get("PROC-01").version, "2.0")
        self.assertTrue(eng.library.get("PROC-01").verified)

    def test_admission_is_idempotent(self):
        eng = engine_with_v2()
        self.assertEqual(apply_proposal(eng, PROPOSAL_V2)["status"], "already_admitted")

    def test_a_weaker_candidate_is_rejected(self):
        """A proposal that keeps the predicates but not the condition they govern."""
        eng = Engine()
        weak = PROC_V2.clone()
        weak.combine = "P6 OR (P1 AND P2 AND P5)"     # P3/P4 present but unreferenced
        r = verify_program(eng, weak)
        self.assertFalse(r["verified"])
        self.assertIn("iv-missing-01", r["failures"])

    def test_every_program_expression_parses_and_normalises(self):
        eng = Engine()
        for pid, versions in eng.library.versions.items():
            for prog in versions:
                node = parse_expr(prog.combine)
                self.assertTrue(node.preds(), pid)
                again = str(parse_expr(str(node)))
                self.assertEqual(str(node), again, f"{pid} @ {prog.version}")
                for p in node.preds():
                    self.assertTrue(any(x.id == p for x in prog.predicates),
                                    f"{pid}: expression names {p}, which is not a predicate")

    def test_before_after_shows_the_fix(self):
        eng = engine_with_v2()
        ba = before_after(eng)
        self.assertEqual(ba["1.0"]["interventions_passed"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(ba["2.0"]["interventions_passed"], 5)
        self.assertFalse(ba["1.0"]["verified"])
        self.assertTrue(ba["2.0"]["verified"])

    def test_out_of_scope_intervention_uses_the_router(self):
        eng = engine_with_v2()
        iv = next(i for i in program_interventions("PROC-01") if i.id == "iv-scope-01")
        self.assertFalse(iv.pinned)
        r = run_intervention(eng, iv, eng.library.get("PROC-01"))
        self.assertTrue(r["pass"])
        self.assertEqual(r["got"], "ESCALATE")

    def test_all_interventions_in_the_library_belong_to_a_program(self):
        for iv in program_interventions("PROC-01"):
            self.assertEqual(iv.program, "PROC-01")
        counts = {pid: len(program_interventions(pid))
                  for pid in ("PROC-01", "DPA-01", "EMERG-01")}
        self.assertEqual(counts, {"PROC-01": 5, "DPA-01": 4, "EMERG-01": 3})


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- bench

class TestArms(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.eng = engine_with_v2()
        cls.arms = run_all(cls.eng)
        cls.econ = economics(cls.eng, cls.arms)

    def test_every_labelled_question_is_answered_correctly_by_the_library(self):
        s = self.arms["B3"]["score"]
        self.assertEqual(s["correct"], s["n"], [r["id"] for r in self.arms["B3"]["rows"]
                                                if r["canon"] != r["expect_canon"]])
        self.assertEqual(s["material_errors"], 0)

    def test_single_pass_is_worse_and_overreaches(self):
        b1 = self.arms["B1"]["score"]
        self.assertLess(b1["correct"], self.arms["B3"]["score"]["correct"])
        self.assertGreater(b1["overreach"], 0)

    def test_frontier_arm_is_flagged_as_modelled(self):
        row = self.arms["A"]["rows"][0]
        self.assertIn("modelled", " ".join(row["notes"]))
        self.assertEqual(self.arms["A"]["score"]["citation_support"], 0.0)

    def test_targeted_retrieval_does_less_work(self):
        self.assertLess(self.arms["B3"]["score"]["avg_predicates"],
                        self.arms["B2"]["score"]["avg_predicates"])
        self.assertLess(self.arms["B3"]["score"]["avg_cost_answered"],
                        self.arms["B2"]["score"]["avg_cost_answered"])

    def test_canonical_labels_unify_the_programs(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.eng.library.get("DPA-01").canon("CLEARED"), "YES")
        self.assertEqual(self.eng.library.get("EMERG-01").canon("NEEDS PRIOR APPROVAL"), "NO")

    def test_localisation_names_a_component(self):
        q = next(x for x in LABELLED if x["id"] == "q05")
        loc = localise(self.eng, q)
        self.assertEqual(loc["status"], "decomposition")

    def test_scope_failure_is_reported_as_scope(self):
        q = next(x for x in LABELLED if x["id"] == "q10")
        self.assertEqual(localise(self.eng, q)["status"], "scope")

    def test_break_even_is_a_number(self):
        self.assertGreater(self.econ["break_even_questions"], 1)
        self.assertLess(self.econ["break_even_questions"], 20)
        self.assertGreater(self.econ["ratio"], 100)

    def test_escalations_are_counted_separately(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.econ["escalations"], 1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(self.econ["escalation_rate"], 1 / 15, places=3)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reuse

class TestReuse(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.eng = engine_with_v2()
        cls.arms = run_all(cls.eng)
        cls.ex = reuse_experiment(cls.eng, economics(cls.eng, cls.arms))

    def test_build_and_held_out_sets_are_disjoint(self):
        self.assertFalse(set(BUILD_SET) & set(HELD_OUT))
        self.assertEqual(len(BUILD_SET) + len(HELD_OUT), len(LABELLED))

    def test_saving_persists_on_unseen_questions(self):
        self.assertGreater(self.ex["held_out_ratio"], 100)
        self.assertLess(self.ex["held_out_cost_per_answered"],
                        self.ex["frontier_per_question"] / 100)

    def test_every_fact_change_lands(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.ex["variants_passed"], 5, [
            v["id"] for v in self.ex["variants"] if not v["pass"]])

    def test_no_rebuild_is_needed(self):
        for v in self.ex["variants"]:
            self.assertFalse(v["rebuilt"])
            self.assertEqual(v["build_cost"], 0.0)

    def test_rule_change_moves_only_the_affected_question(self):
        v = next(v for v in self.ex["variants"] if v["id"] == "rule-reverted")
        changed = sorted(r["id"] for r in v["changed"])
        self.assertEqual(changed, ["q01"])

    def test_savings_curve_is_monotonic(self):
        rows = self.ex["savings"]
        ratios = [r["ratio"] for r in rows]
        self.assertEqual(ratios, sorted(ratios))
        self.assertLess(ratios[0], 1)      # not worth it for one question
        self.assertGreater(ratios[-1], 50)  # worth it for many


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- server + ui

class TestServer(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        from jig import server
        cls.server_mod = server
        server.STATE.reset()
        cls.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), server.Handler)
        cls.port = cls.httpd.server_address[1]
        cls.thread = threading.Thread(target=cls.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        cls.thread.start()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.httpd.shutdown()
        cls.httpd.server_close()
        cls.server_mod.STATE.reset()

    def get(self, path):
        with urllib.request.urlopen(f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.port}{path}", timeout=60) as r:
            return json.loads(r.read())

    def post(self, path, body):
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.port}{path}", data=json.dumps(body).encode(),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as r:
            return json.loads(r.read())

    def test_health(self):
        self.assertTrue(self.get("/api/health")["ok"])

    def test_state_carries_the_labels(self):
        s = self.get("/api/state")
        self.assertIn("MODELLED", s["labels"]["cost_model"])
        self.assertEqual(s["judge"]["name"], "reference")

    def test_decide_returns_a_decision(self):
        q = "Can we approve a $4,200 analytics subscription from Acme Analytics on " \
            "2026-11-05, charged to ENG-TOOLS?"
        d = self.post("/api/decide", {"question": q})["decision"]
        self.assertIn(d["label"], {"APPROVE", "DENY", "DEFER", "ESCALATE"})
        self.assertTrue(d["trace"])

    def test_decide_rejects_an_empty_question(self):
        with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as ctx:
            self.post("/api/decide", {"question": "  "})
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, 400)

    def test_bench_and_verify_are_served(self):
        b = self.get("/api/bench")
        self.assertIn("economics", b)
        self.assertEqual(len(b["arms"]), 4)
        v = self.get("/api/verify")
        self.assertTrue(v["programs"])

    def test_admit_flow_over_http(self):
        res = self.post("/api/admit", {"proposal": "PR-1"})
        self.assertEqual(res["status"], "admitted")
        lib = self.get("/api/library")
        proc = next(l for l in lib["library"] if l["program"]["id"] == "PROC-01")
        self.assertEqual(proc["active"], "2.0")

    def test_unknown_endpoint_is_404(self):
        with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as ctx:
            self.get("/api/nope")
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, 404)


class TestStaticPage(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
                            "web", "app.html")
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            cls.html = f.read()

    def test_no_external_resources(self):
        # the preview iframe has no network: nothing may be fetched from a CDN
        self.assertIsNone(re.search(r'(src|href)\s*=\s*"https?://', self.html))
        self.assertNotIn("@import", self.html)

    def test_snapshot_is_baked(self):
        self.assertNotIn("/*__SNAPSHOT__*/ null", self.html)
        m = re.search(r"const SNAPSHOT = (\{.*?\});\n", self.html, re.S)
        self.assertIsNotNone(m)
        snap = json.loads(m.group(1))
        for ep in ("/api/state", "/api/verify", "/api/bench", "/api/reuse"):
            self.assertIn(ep, snap)
        decide = [k for k in snap if k.startswith("/api/decide:")]
        self.assertEqual(len(decide), 8)

    def test_all_views_present(self):
        for view in ("decide", "programs", "goggles", "arms", "reuse", "method"):
            self.assertIn(f'data-view="{view}"', self.html)

    def test_snapshot_has_no_placeholder_numbers(self):
        m = re.search(r"const SNAPSHOT = (\{.*?\});\n", self.html, re.S)
        snap = json.loads(m.group(1))
        bench = snap["/api/bench"]
        self.assertGreater(bench["economics"]["ratio"], 100)
        self.assertEqual(bench["arms"]["B3"]["score"]["material_errors"], 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: verify.py
================================================
"""
jig.verify — the interventions that decide whether a program is admitted.

Agreement with a frontier answer is not evidence. A pipeline can produce the
right verdict for the wrong reason, and a wrong verdict with a confident
citation looks identical from the outside. So a program version earns its place
in the library by surviving interventions on the corpus:

  flip a decisive fact            the outcome must change
  reword irrelevantly             the outcome and its citations must not move
  remove indispensable evidence   the program must refuse to decide (DEFER)
  change the governing rule       the program must follow the version in force
  leave the library's scope       the program must escalate, not guess

The third one is the one that finds missing predicates. A program that never
needed a source cannot miss it, so it keeps answering after the source is gone —
which is exactly how PROC-01 v1.0 gets caught.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from .corpus import Corpus, DEFAULT
from .core import Program

BASE_Q01 = ("Can we approve a $4,200 analytics subscription from Acme Analytics "
            "on 2026-11-05, charged to ENG-TOOLS?")
BASE_Q11 = ("Can we onboard Orbit CRM for crm data on 2026-11-05?")
BASE_OLLIE = ("Can we onboard Ollie Labs for analytics on 2026-11-05?")
BASE_Q14 = ("Can we proceed without prior approval on an emergency purchase of "
            "$9,000 of ventilation safety equipment on 2026-11-05, CFO notified "
            "within 24 hours: yes?")
LEASE = "Should we renew the office lease for our Boston office in 2027?"


@dataclass
class Intervention:
    id: str
    program: str
    kind: str
    title: str
    rationale: str
    question: str
    expect: str                    # expected verdict label
    expect_citations: list[str] | None = None   # or an exact citation set
    setup: object = None           # callable(Corpus) -> Corpus
    slots: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    pinned: bool = True            # False -> let routing decide (tests the router)

    def stage(self, corpus: Corpus) -> Corpus:
        return self.setup(corpus) if self.setup else corpus


def _suspend_acme(c: Corpus) -> Corpus:
    return c.replace_text("registry/vendors",
                          "Acme Analytics — status: approved",
                          "Acme Analytics — status: suspended")


def _drop(corpus: Corpus, doc_id: str):
    return lambda c: c.remove(doc_id)


def _rewrite(doc_id: str, old: str, new: str):
    return lambda c: c.replace_text(doc_id, old, new)


def _chatter(c: Corpus) -> Corpus:
    return c


CHATTER = (" This request was discussed in the Tuesday sync; the manager prefers "
           "the blue dashboard theme and asked for it to be routed early.")


INTERVENTIONS: list[Intervention] = [
    # ---------------- PROC-01 ----------------
    Intervention(
        "iv-flip-01", "PROC-01", "flip_decisive_fact",
        "Suspend the vendor: the outcome must change",
        "The vendor register is the decisive source for the vendor condition. "
        "Suspending the vendor must flip APPROVE to DENY, not merely lower a "
        "confidence.",
        BASE_Q01, "DENY", setup=_suspend_acme),
    Intervention(
        "iv-irrelevant-01", "PROC-01", "irrelevant_rewording",
        "Add irrelevant chatter: outcome and citations must not move",
        "Filler about meeting notes and colour preferences shares no vocabulary "
        "with any source. If it changes the decision or the citation set, the "
        "pipeline is reacting to surface form.",
        BASE_Q01 + CHATTER, "APPROVE",
        expect_citations=["policy/procurement-2.0", "policy/data-taxonomy",
                          "registry/budget", "registry/security",
                          "registry/vendors"]),
    Intervention(
        "iv-missing-01", "PROC-01", "remove_indispensable_evidence",
        "Delete the security review register: must DEFER",
        "Policy v2.0 §5 makes the review register indispensable for a vendor that "
        "processes personal data. With the register gone the honest outcome is "
        "DEFER plus a request for it. A program with no review predicate cannot "
        "notice the loss and will keep answering — that is what this catches.",
        BASE_Q01, "DEFER", setup=_drop(DEFAULT, "registry/security")),
    Intervention(
        "iv-rule-01", "PROC-01", "governing_rule_changed",
        "Ask the same question before v2.0 took effect: must follow v1.4",
        "At 2026-05-01 the manager limit is $2,500 and the $4,200 request is above "
        "it. The decision must follow the policy version in force on the request "
        "date, not the newest file in the corpus.",
        ("Can we approve a $4,200 analytics subscription from Acme Analytics on "
         "2026-05-01, charged to ENG-TOOLS?"), "DENY"),
    Intervention(
        "iv-scope-01", "PROC-01", "out_of_scope",
        "Ask about an office lease: must escalate",
        "No program in the library covers property leases. The pipeline must say "
        "so rather than route the question to the nearest-looking program.",
        LEASE, "ESCALATE", pinned=False),

    # ---------------- DPA-01 ----------------
    Intervention(
        "iv-flip-11", "DPA-01", "flip_decisive_fact",
        "Remove the executed addendum: must BLOCK",
        "The only thing standing between an analytics vendor and clearance is the "
        "executed addendum.",
        BASE_Q11, "BLOCKED", setup=_drop(DEFAULT, "contracts/msa-orbit")),
    Intervention(
        "iv-irrelevant-11", "DPA-01", "irrelevant_rewording",
        "Add chatter to the onboarding question: must not move",
        "Same invariance test on a second program.", BASE_Q11 + CHATTER, "CLEARED"),
    Intervention(
        "iv-missing-11", "DPA-01", "remove_indispensable_evidence",
        "Delete the data taxonomy on a vendor with no addendum: must DEFER",
        "For Orbit the addendum alone settles clearance, so losing the taxonomy "
        "correctly does not move the answer. For a vendor with no addendum it is "
        "decisive: with the taxonomy the answer is BLOCKED, without it there is "
        "no way to know whether the category is in scope, so the honest answer "
        "becomes DEFER. This intervention uses the second question on purpose.",
        BASE_OLLIE, "DEFER", setup=_drop(DEFAULT, "policy/data-taxonomy")),
    Intervention(
        "iv-scope-11", "DPA-01", "out_of_scope",
        "Lease question routed to the onboarding program: must escalate",
        "Routing must be able to decline.", LEASE, "ESCALATE", pinned=False),

    # ---------------- EMERG-01 ----------------
    Intervention(
        "iv-flip-14", "EMERG-01", "flip_decisive_fact",
        "CFO was not notified: must NEED PRIOR APPROVAL",
        "The notification fact is decisive for the emergency procedure.",
        BASE_Q14.replace("yes?", "no?"), "NEEDS PRIOR APPROVAL"),
    Intervention(
        "iv-rule-14", "EMERG-01", "governing_rule_changed",
        "Same request for software: must fall back to prior approval",
        "The procedure excludes software and services. Reusing the program on a "
        "licence purchase must not carry the emergency exemption across.",
        ("Can we proceed without prior approval on an emergency purchase of "
         "$9,000 of software licences on 2026-11-05, CFO notified within 24 "
         "hours: yes?"), "NEEDS PRIOR APPROVAL"),
    Intervention(
        "iv-irrelevant-14", "EMERG-01", "irrelevant_rewording",
        "Add chatter to the emergency question: must not move",
        "Same invariance test on the third program.",
        BASE_Q14 + CHATTER, "PROCEED"),
]


def program_interventions(pid: str) -> list[Intervention]:
    return [i for i in INTERVENTIONS if i.program == pid]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# running them
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_intervention(engine, iv: Intervention, prog: Program,
                     base_corpus: Corpus | None = None) -> dict:
    base = base_corpus or engine.corpus
    corpus = iv.stage(base)
    d = (engine.run(iv.question, program=prog, corpus=corpus) if iv.pinned
         else engine.run(iv.question, corpus=corpus))

    ok = d.label == iv.expect
    detail = ""
    if ok and iv.expect_citations is not None:
        got = sorted({c.doc_id for c in d.citations})
        want = sorted(iv.expect_citations)
        ok = got == want
        detail = ("" if ok else f"citations moved: {got} != {want}")
    if not ok and not detail:
        detail = f"expected {iv.expect}, got {d.label}"

    return {
        "id": iv.id, "program": iv.program, "kind": iv.kind, "title": iv.title,
        "rationale": iv.rationale, "question": iv.question,
        "expect": iv.expect, "got": d.label, "pass": ok, "detail": detail,
        "verdict": d.verdict, "citations": [c.doc_id for c in d.citations],
        "evaluated": d.evaluated, "skipped": len(d.skipped),
        "gaps": d.gaps, "cost": d.cost["total"],
    }


def verify_program(engine, prog: Program, labelled: list[dict] | None = None,
                   base_corpus: Corpus | None = None) -> dict:
    """All interventions for this program, plus regression on its labelled set."""
    program_id, version = prog.id, prog.version
    ivs = program_interventions(program_id)
    results = [run_intervention(engine, iv, prog, base_corpus) for iv in ivs]

    reg = []
    if labelled is None:
        from .programs import labelled_by_program
        labelled = labelled_by_program(program_id)
    for q in labelled:
        d = engine.run(q["question"], program=prog,
                       corpus=base_corpus or engine.corpus)
        reg.append({"id": q["id"], "expect": q["expect"], "got": d.label,
                    "pass": d.label == q["expect"], "question": q["question"],
                    "dec_and": q["decisive"]})

    n_iv = len(results)
    n_iv_pass = sum(1 for r in results if r["pass"])
    n_reg_pass = sum(1 for r in reg if r["pass"])
    verified = n_iv_pass == n_iv and n_reg_pass == len(reg)
    return {
        "program": program_id, "version": version,
        "interventions": results, "regression": reg,
        "interventions_passed": n_iv_pass, "interventions_total": n_iv,
        "regression_passed": n_reg_pass, "regression_total": len(reg),
        "verified": verified,
        "failures": [r["id"] for r in results if not r["pass"]] +
                    [r["id"] for r in reg if not r["pass"]],
    }


def verify_all(engine, base_corpus: Corpus | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for pid, versions in engine.library.versions.items():
        for p in versions:
            out.append(verify_program(engine, p, base_corpus=base_corpus))
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# the second role of the frontier model: propose, then be verified
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def apply_proposal(engine, proposal: dict) -> dict:
    """Build the proposed version, verify it, admit it only if it passes."""
    from .programs import PROC_V2

    if proposal["proposed_version"] != PROC_V2.version:
        raise KeyError(proposal["proposed_version"])

    lib = engine.library
    if lib.get("PROC-01", PROC_V2.version) is not None and \
            any(p.version == PROC_V2.version for p in lib.versions.get("PROC-01", [])):
        return {"status": "already_admitted", "proposal": proposal["id"]}

    candidate = PROC_V2.clone()
    v = verify_program(engine, candidate, base_corpus=engine.corpus)
    v["stage"] = "candidate"
    if not v["verified"]:
        return {"status": "rejected", "proposal": proposal["id"], "verification": v}

    candidate.verified = True
    candidate.verification = {
        "interventions_passed": v["interventions_passed"],
        "interventions_total": v["interventions_total"],
        "regression_passed": v["regression_passed"],
        "regression_total": v["regression_total"],
        "admitted": True,
    }
    candidate.proposals = [proposal]
    lib.add(candidate, activate=True)
    lib.proposals = [p for p in lib.proposals if p["id"] != proposal["id"]]
    return {"status": "admitted", "proposal": proposal["id"],
            "verification": v, "active": candidate.version}


def before_after(engine) -> dict:
    """v1.0 vs the proposed v2.0 on the same questions — the localisation view."""
    out = {}
    for version in ("1.0", "2.0"):
        if not any(p.version == version for p in engine.library.versions.get("PROC-01", [])):
            continue
        prog = next(p for p in engine.library.versions["PROC-01"] if p.version == version)
        v = verify_program(engine, prog)
        out[version] = {
            "interventions_passed": v["interventions_passed"],
            "interventions_total": v["interventions_total"],
            "regression_passed": v["regression_passed"],
            "regression_total": v["regression_total"],
            "failures": v["failures"],
            "verified": v["verified"],
        }
    return out
